#### Next bounded recovery-owner contract — reviewed preparation

**Historical source card:** this private source is now authored. The current
checkpoint above owns its results and remaining qualification; its next WSL
preparation is the invocation-level worker stop/completion contract.

The coordinator, `/root/recovery_contract_review` and
`/root/native_source_independent` reviewed this next source contract under
REQ-8/BND-2 and CHECK-6/8/9/10/11/15/21/25/27. Complete EP4's selected source
checkpoint accounting before dependent implementation. Native/product gates
remain open; this record authorizes no repeated native/public operation or
Core-positive receiving claim. It refines the existing receiving-boundary child,
not a new plan or a shipped retry policy.

**Actual owner.** Extend `run`'s composition boundary with immutable admitted
`Config`, one `ProcessClaim`, one `ProviderScope`, invocation-owned route backoff
and aggregate accounting. Each admitted transaction receives a fresh Control;
association retirement precedes provider retirement. Never retry `run`, reset
process admission, reopen committed control or shut down CNG per transaction.
Public execution stays one-shot. Private repeated Script controls must reach
the shared producer/receiver path rather than a disconnected retry helper.

**Original cutoff.** Freeze the absolute monotonic invocation cutoff once.
Experimental Control construction clamps handshake and total deadlines to it
without extending existing stage/context maxima. Strict-before checks at
admission, I/O waits, publication and final commit retain that original cutoff.
Checking admission alone permits overruns. Expiry closes admission but supplies
no physical-completion fact.

| Actual producer outcome | Selected bounded disposition |
| --- | --- |
| `establish` primary native Connect/Handshake/Request/Response with TimedOut, ConnectionRefused, ConnectionReset or ConnectionAborted | Candidate later KE reprobe only through this exact receiver and every scheduling guard. Preserve typed-primary precedence. |
| Nonempty `Failed.cleanup` or `admission_cleanup` | Terminal for this child; retain primary and secondary facts separately. |
| Typed TLS/setup/profile/exporter refusal, unclassified cause, other I/O or structural/negotiation refusal | Unavailable; no generic retry, trust inference or speculative correction. |
| Local allocation, entropy, runtime, control or admission failure | Terminal local failure; no server-trust allegation. |
| UDP setup/send/readiness/receive/shutdown failure or Control deadline | No automatic KE route. Separate stage-specific review is required. |
| Rejected protected reply, including authenticated zero-cookie reply | Existing Pending, original deadline and decreased receive allowance continue; no immediate KE or refund. |
| Genuine association `RecoveryIntent` | Inert coalesced scheduling input retaining original not-before; consumption/copying creates no permit. |
| KE or preview success | No backoff reset. |
| Consumed genuine `CommittedProtected` of the currently active transaction | Reset invocation-owned route backoff deterministically; never counters, cutoff or other earliest-operation constraints. |
| Cancellation, cutoff or aggregate exhaustion | Terminal; no replacement context. |
| RATE/DENY/RSTR/NTSN/control input | No newly enabled route; protocol-specific admission/disposition remains a prerequisite. |

**Checked experimental parameters.** Supply explicit finite invocation window,
scheduling-attempt/refusal and diagnostic-work ceilings, KE minimum/maximum
delay, checked growth and bounded jitter. Select no shipped defaults. Maximum
means total delay including jitter; minimum is strictly positive. Commit one
bounded draw with the failure-transition update so notifications cannot redraw.
Growth caps at maximum; unrepresentable arithmetic refuses. Admission honors
the maximum applicable route/source/global/original-intent boundary. Waiting
parks; coalesced events neither replay missed ticks nor replenish budgets.

**Accounting and custody.** Irreversible scheduling/refusal counters are separate
from physical occupancy permits. This child introduces no service `WorkPermit`,
blocking-job channel or completion reservation. Existing backend/runtime
retirement remains mandatory; Drop proves no graceful/native completion. Later
service admission must prevent pre-submit permit leaks explicitly, without
dropping a charged permit as rollback or completing unsubmitted work.

**Observable exit.** Actual shared Script-path sequences must show no early or
excess submission, finite refusal exhaustion, eligible healthy reprobe, exact
cutoff/jitter/overflow behavior, preserved zero-cookie budgets and cleanup facts.
Old, foreign, stale, dropped and preview outcomes cannot reset route state.
Production defaults, KoD recovery, restart/reboot continuity, persistence,
provider/trust/native qualification and Core-positive issuers remain separate
held prerequisites. No material owner decision blocks this bounded preparation.

#### Rust refinements at the receiver freeze — 9 November 2026

The [additional Rust research disposition](../.agents/plans/2026-10-30-skill-quality.md#additional-rust-opportunities--9-november-2026)
retains the following proposals against `a31835d6`. Both are **unimplemented and
unexecuted**; the next product work remains this actual KE-producer/receiver
contract freeze. They add no provider, trust, cookie-policy or execution admission.

**R3-C-01 — directional key API.** Engine association/crypto owners retain
S2.2, REQ-8/BND-2, AC-04 and CHECK-6/8/9/17/21/25/26/27. At the real receiver
freeze, compare one exclusive private pair exposing `seal_request`/`open_response`
without public raw getters against separate non-Clone C2S/S2C owners only where
actual consumers need separate custody. The current laboratory producer already
checks both exporter contexts on the same connection, then destroys its keys
when returning counts; **no existing key swap or product bug was established**.
Preserve that producer provenance, joint association binding, cleanup and current
effect guards. The useful test reaches the real receiver: correct-direction
packet success beside an independently specified wrong-context/wrong-key
authentication refusal with no publication. Exercise constructor/wrong-direction
misuse at the claimed compile-time boundary; types alone cannot validate a private
producer. If the pair removes key selection entirely, do not invent a surrogate
key-parameter API merely to obtain a compile failure.

**R3-R-01 — conditional completion reservation.** Engine/native-producer and
W11-W05/S3.1 lifecycle owners retain CHECK-17/21/27 and THR-26. At this same
worker/coordinator contract freeze, consider Tokio `try_reserve_owned` and
`OwnedPermit` only if the actual design selects a bounded completion channel;
fixed result slots or retained/joined job owners may be simpler for the small
population. Freeze actual job and result-byte limits before submission. Make
admission transactional without unbounded reserve waiters: the existing
`WorkPermit` deliberately keeps its charge
on Drop, so dropping an already-issued permit is not rollback. Refusal before
submission must neither leak that charge nor assert unperformed physical
completion. Channel capacity, physical completion, receiver acceptance and
secret/result retirement remain separate events with their own custody.

`Receiver::close()` still permits reserved sends and needs draining to `None`;
receiver drop is a different failure. `OwnedPermit::send` supplies neither a
consumption acknowledgement nor a recoverable unsent result. A permit retained
by stuck native work can keep draining incomplete; preserve diagnosed shutdown/
process recovery. The planned test must reach actual admission/retirement code:
hold one job beyond cancellation beside a healthy accepted neighbor, refuse work
beyond the frozen bound, observe retained bytes and physical completion
independently, and reject the stale result without publication. Cover failed
pre-submission admission, receiver close/drop and worker panic separately. A
standalone channel demonstration establishes neither this integration nor native
termination.

#### Tokio refinements at the KE boundary — 9 November 2026

Research against source baseline `33a4a44d` (unchanged by the following R4 docs
commit `e74fb270`) identified three conditional refinements for this same S2.2
producer/receiver freeze. **No current runtime defect or executed integration
result was established.** The actual engine privately drives current-thread
Tokio 1.54.1 with I/O/time, `max_blocking_threads(2)` and two owned control timers;
it submits no production blocking KE jobs. The laboratory producer currently
uses synchronous numeric-address TCP/Rustls and returns counts after disposal.
Use [the Tokio reference](../.agents/skills/implementation/implement-rust/references/tokio.md)
and its pinned source register; this selects no async TLS adapter or new feature.

| Refinement / existing owner | Contract to freeze and smallest meaningful later control |
| --- | --- |
| **T-R-01 — driver lifetime.** S2.2 complete KE worker/coordinator; W11-W05/S3.1 for lifecycle integration; CHECK-27 and resource CHECK-21. | If the worker uses `Handle::block_on`, name the owner keeping the same current-thread `Runtime::block_on` active for dependent I/O/timers through stop/retirement. A cloned Handle or `enter` is insufficient. At the real selected worker seam, hold a loopback I/O/timer dependency, request stop, release it and observe terminal retirement beside healthy completion. A deliberately retired-driver control needs an independent finite watchdog. The wholly synchronous route is a valid alternative. |
| **T-R-02 — shared blocking-pool progress.** Same worker plus actual resolver/submission owner; §17.1/THR-26, CHECK-21/27. | Do not occupy all worker threads with outer closures awaiting nested jobs queued to that pool, including hidden DNS. Independent deadlines may release outer waits; account separately for any remaining queued/started work. Pre-resolution retains destination/path re-admission. At the actual submission seam, saturate the admitted outer population and show the selected bounded progress/refusal route and truthful work custody; pair a healthy numeric-address exchange. A generic deadlock demo or increased cap alone does not qualify this integration. |
| **TIO-01 — strict expiry at acceptance.** Same-connection producer and serialized association/result receiver; CHECK-6/9/25/27, CHECK-21 when observing retained resources. | Retain original stage/total deadlines within the operation. `timeout_at` success grants no timely publication: test a valid pre-expiry candidate beside one immediately ready at/after expiry, and a poll starting before but completing after. Include fragmented progress crossing original expiry and policy/generation cutover before commit. Observe actual publication refusal and retained/retired custody, not just the wrapper result. Current UDP/series final guards already enforce this distinction. |

These controls extend the existing R3 admission/retirement and receiver cases;
do not start parallel campaigns or replace their actual consumer with a model.
Any new dependency/feature selection still needs CHECK-26 graph admission;
diagnostic/secret outputs retain CHECK-17. The feature pin, provider/trust gates, finite-limit
decisions, all Windows/fuzz/storage/UI holds and post-V1 formal deferral remain.

For an eventual async KE transport, apply the existing
[§5.4.4 framing rule](../docs/windows-time-architecture.md#544-framing-cancellation-and-native-completion):
retain header/body/write progress across resumable cancellation or retire the
connection. Reuse the paired split/coalesced/truncated/partial-write controls
through the actual selected transport. This is a cross-reference to an existing
invariant, not a newly found synchronous-lab defect.

**TIO-02 — later production polling trigger only.** If the actual S2.1 scheduler
selects Interval, explicitly choose first-fire, missed-tick and resume behavior;
`interval` fires first immediately and defaults to Burst. The inspected version's
missed-tick strategies apply only beyond 5 ms lateness. Skip/Delay alone is no
packet-rate policy; retain burst/backoff bounds, current generations and final
send eligibility, with adaptive one-shot `sleep_until` still an alternative.
When that consumer exists, delay it across nominal periods and observe actual
packet times/counts, invalidation and healthy-source progress under CHECK-21/27;
W11-W02/W11-W01 and CHECK-11 own native suspend/mapping evidence. There is no current engine
Interval consumer. This later trigger does not precede or displace S2.2's freeze.

[`lib.rs`](../crates/nevrotick-engine/src/lib.rs),
[`series.rs`](../crates/nevrotick-engine/src/series.rs), focused test sources and
the isolated synchronous KE laboratory. The relevant files were unchanged from
`6af28b653b93640c5550483b2fc72cb63bdea33c`, used for the independent source passes.
The inspected owners retain private current-thread I/O/time drivers,
entered-runtime rejection, nonblocking socket import, bounded readiness/receive
work, fresh control/deadline checks and abort-plus-await timer cleanup. No current
Tokio correctness defect or additional-feature requirement was demonstrated.
Tests were inspected, not rerun; this supplies no native, performance or
service-stop result.

The thread cap is not a production KE admission/completion system. Carry that
integration through **R3-R-01**, **T-R-01/T-R-02** and **TIO-01** above;
W11-W02 retains native resume/wakeup qualification and W11-W05/S3.1 owns service
lifecycle integration. [H-ET1](#bug-hunt-scheduling-assessment--h-et1) retains the
bounded receive-before-write behavior and unsent-neighbor fairness question;
alternating peers does not supply a stronger send-progress guarantee. Reuse these
owners before adding Tokio helpers or changing the scheduler. V1 isolation,
admission, physical-completion custody and responsive control remain required;
[MT-0](../docs/plans/post-v1-multithreading.md) keeps its post-V1 activation.
This review creates no new implementation stage or execution permission.

wakes. The native resume-to-generation/control path remains to be integrated and

and stale-publication controls; the 30-second lab cap is no service-stop guarantee.

association outcome, named trust-profile basis, packet authentication/current
completion, numerical eligibility/refusal and Core insufficient/ready/rejected
outcome. Include allowlisted stage/reason, provenance/age and resource/retry results;

and [Windows continuation](windows11-integration.md#430-ordinary-s22-windows-qualification--5-november-2026)
own OPEN-8 and CHECK-9/26/27. Keep CNG-first/Schannel-second candidate order.

Neither may block the responsive event-loop/control owner; a synchronous verifier

the actual job until completion; cancellation removes publication eligibility,
not capacity accounting. No replacement pools or detached retry growth.

for stalled-work stop/escalation. Inventory actual delegated PKI fetch owners;
the NTS socket’s Direct binding cannot establish their egress.

#### Verbatim independent review

> The change prepares the ordinary S2.2 acquisition path: a normal-date, certificate-validated NTS-KE transaction produces one complete association, that association protects a UDP exchange, and separately admitted time evidence reaches the real numerical consumer. It assigns the missing runtime transitions to the existing engine, protocol, crypto and Windows owners, with useful and refused controls at each boundary. It changes documentation and current-state routing; it does not implement these transitions or select the outstanding provider, trust profile, resource values or zero-replacement-cookie policy. The principal handoff is `tasks/engine-transport-pilot.md`, “Ordinary full NTS composition — S2.2 preparation, 5 November 2026.”
>
> The inspected workspace already contains six crates, inert NTS wire/negotiation kernels, a real two-peer loopback transport observer and a separate fixture exchange-to-Core chain. The transport currently ends in `TransportFacts`; it has no domain/Core dependency or authenticated association. Pure leap knowledge is present, and the existing settled-rate receipt records completion only for four Linux fixture owners. Production eligibility, the numerical bridge and their remaining proofs are still planned. `docs/plans/development-plan.md` §7 controls the current proof continuation; Slint is selected but its migration remains separately gated, Direct is the first-delivery route, SOCKS5 is deferred, and provider/native/storage/fuzz holds remain effective.
>
> ## Scoped verdict
>
> **READY for integration as the ordinary S2.2 documentation/research handoff at the frozen candidate identified below.** No material finding was established in this bounded review. The producer, state owner, consumer and future qualification relationships are coherent, and the inspected source and primary requirements support the consequential new assertions. This verdict grants no implementation admission, native experiment, product-security qualification, P2 closure or release approval.

> ### Primary external evidence actually inspected
>
> - [RFC 8915](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8915.html), the affected KE completion, record, endpoint-default, size-guidance, retry, exporter and protected-packet/cookie requirements in §§4–5.7.
> - [RFC 8446 §6.1](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8446.html#section-6.1), closure-alert requirements, and the regular-versus-early exporter distinction associated with §7.5.
> - [RFC 5297 §§3 and 6.1](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc5297.html#section-3), nonce placement and algorithm-15 key/nonce lengths.
> - [Chrony pinned `nts_ntp_client.c`](https://github.com/mlichvar/chrony/blob/8df4f1263e206585e6c4e61352d042c2dd12916a/nts_ntp_client.c), lines 200–225 and 404–547: saved-address fallback, cookie extraction and response-authentication completion.
> - [ntpd-rs pinned response consumer](https://github.com/pendulum-project/ntpd-rs/blob/46ec9bb4d5b6cb24f814f5543d85b9138afb4cba/ntp-proto/src/source.rs), lines 759–836; [KE result construction](https://github.com/pendulum-project/ntpd-rs/blob/46ec9bb4d5b6cb24f814f5543d85b9138afb4cba/ntp-proto/src/nts/mod.rs), lines 363–420; and [spawner resolution](https://github.com/pendulum-project/ntpd-rs/blob/46ec9bb4d5b6cb24f814f5543d85b9138afb4cba/ntpd/src/daemon/spawn/nts.rs), lines 124–165.
> - [Pinned rustls-platform-verifier Windows source](https://github.com/rustls/rustls-platform-verifier/blob/252e25161a91af476cbea620e29d277592d27ec7/rustls-platform-verifier/src/verification/windows.rs), bounded slices covering the SSL-policy flags, default engine/store, chain flags and timeout, date conversion, policy-status check and `ServerCertVerified` return.
> - Microsoft contracts for [CertGetCertificateChain](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/wincrypt/nf-wincrypt-certgetcertificatechain), [CertVerifyCertificateChainPolicy](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/wincrypt/nf-wincrypt-certverifycertificatechainpolicy), [system-store locations](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/seccrypto/system-store-locations), [CryptRetrieveObjectByUrlW](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/wincrypt/nf-wincrypt-cryptretrieveobjectbyurlw), and [BCryptGenerateSymmetricKey](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/bcrypt/nf-bcrypt-bcryptgeneratesymmetrickey).
>
> ## Material findings
>
> **None established.** No `S22-R` finding ID is allocated. The following are the principal checks supporting that scoped result, rather than additional approval gates or claims of executed behavior.
>
> | Reviewed boundary | Assessment and evidence |
> | --- | --- |
> | Current state versus planned system | The new capsule and engine card correctly describe absent authenticated acquisition and the production numerical bridge. The manifests and actual `TransportFacts` producer support this distinction. The settled-rate correction is limited to the receipt's four-owner Linux fixture scope; it does not close OD-10 or P1. |
> | KE parsing and exporter authority | Engine handoff steps 2–3 match the existing accumulator/negotiation API boundaries. `NtsKeExporterContext` owns five context bytes and a label but neither a connection nor keys. Requiring same-job binding, both 32-byte direction keys and complete publication prevents the handoff from treating inert metadata as authentication. Error/non-error cardinality and TLS closure remain explicitly distinguished. |
> | RFC details and upstream differences | The size target retains SHOULD strength, and the closure wording does not invent a mandatory peer-alert wait. The zero-replacement-cookie distinction is accurate: the server's response obligation is separate from the explicit client UID/authentication checks. The cited Chrony extractor rejects when no acceptable replacement is extracted; the inspected ntpd-rs consumer processes new cookies after acceptance. The document leaves NevroTick's acceptance/completion/retry disposition pending instead of selecting policy from this difference. |
> | Endpoint and replacement ownership | Step 3 retains the actual KE peer IP when no server is negotiated and port 123 when absent, consistent with §7.4 and RFC 8915 §§4.1.7–4.1.8. New address/family progress needs its own valid KE/delegation provenance. Private candidate construction, complete publication and independently valid old-state retention agree with the existing address-family and Direct contracts. |
> | Packet completion and numerical eligibility | Steps 4–6 keep fresh UID issuance, exclusive cookie ownership, protected send, complete packet admission and consume-once state at identified owners. The current envelope reader intentionally lacks those semantic guarantees. The paired controls retain a healthy pending reply after malformed/uncorrelated traffic, while authentic numerical refusal creates no vote. The actual fixture consumer's `1.0` divisor is correctly excluded as production uncertainty evidence. |
> | Windows trust and native work | The profile freeze and truthful opaque-result language match both the inspected verifier and §18.7.2. A null default chain engine is current-user context; successful API execution is separate from successful chain-policy status. The source checks leaf revocation, suppresses unknown-revocation policy errors and exposes an opaque success marker. The handoff does not convert this into confirmed non-revocation or automatically require a new verifier. Real-job permit/input custody, queued-job bounds and delegated-fetch ownership remain with §17.1 and W11 owners. |
> | Stage and authority | Development §7 keeps the current proof queue. The new Windows card distinguishes a declared ordinary-validation lab path from final installed-service qualification. No complete-MSIX or Slint prerequisite is added to the bounded S2.2 prototype; no existing installed-product gate is marked complete. OD-11's Direct/SOCKS split and OD-05's optional enhanced provenance remain intact. |
>
> ## Evidence limits and retained open work
>
> This review performed read-only Git/hash checks, documentation/source inspection and bounded primary-source retrieval. It did not run Cargo, native Windows, mathematical-proof, fuzz, interoperability or resource campaigns; install anything; alter trust stores; contact NTS peers; modify the candidate; or publish anything. The raw report is the only output written.
>
> The review establishes consistency of the named handoff, not whole-repository correctness, complete source coverage, dependency/licence clearance or an independently repeated historical qualification. The partial snapshot's unrelated omissions are outside the finding set. I did not establish current RFC verified-errata coverage; the candidate already states that limit without claiming that no errata exist.

**Reference and weighting are substantive seams.** The retained Chrony
`ntp_core.c` at lines 2161–2199 uses midpoint/precision/capture/frequency treatment
under stated timing assumptions. The retained `sourcestats.c` at lines 528–550
recomputes squared dimensionless divisors from the current window's peer
distances, minimum/median and local precision. Their retained bytes were rechecked:
`ntp_core.c` matches the existing [S0.1 source ledger](s0.1-chrony-ledger.json);
`sourcestats.c` matches
the [S1.2 port record](s1.2-regression-readiness.md#exact-primary-sources-and-licence-scope).
These are adaptation evidence, not selected NevroTick physical thresholds.
The next statistical contract must preserve that whole-window dependence or
independently justify a different algorithm; it cannot copy the formula into a
permanently stored incoming divisor. Do not substitute fixture divisor `1.0`.

For the existing nominal result, if local conversion remainders are `r1,r4`
over `10^9`, then the exact representation corrections are:

```text
phase_seconds = [phase_q33 - (r1 + r4)/10^9] / 2^33
raw_rtt_seconds = [rtt_q32 + (r4 - r1)/10^9] / 2^32
```

These corrections are known representation effects, not physical uncertainty.
The divisions in this specification are rational, with no implied integer rounding.
They also differ from the later ties-even nanosecond residual over `2^33`.
Retain the original acquisition identity/reference through every transformation;
later evaluation grows age and rechecks generations without creating freshness.

**Actual receiver behavior to preserve.** `FixtureHistory::try_append` returns
`Result<(), HistoryError>`, not a ready estimate. The first and second successful
appends commit observations with `report()==None`. `prepare_fixture_input`
separately reports insufficient count/reference metadata. At capacity it stages
the newest 63 previous rows plus the new one; all normalization/statistics work
precedes the single state assignment. A future production receiver must specify
whether a failed fit rejects that complete append or adopts another explicit
transition. Correlated exchange completion is consumed once even when subsequent
sample admission refuses; no threshold-seeking retry or renewed freshness follows.

The present `FixtureContext`, phase envelope ±1 s, divisors `[1/16,16]`, 3–64
points, 62.5 ms minimum span and 4096 s window are numerical fixture contracts.
They supply no product correction limit, large-recovery policy, independent

every row/count/reference/report on context/order/window refusal and healthy

#### Admission contract refinement — 6 November 2026

Three fresh-context read-only workers completed separate temporal, quality and
receiver questions on clean`2685981`; no Rust/native/formal commands ran. Their
conclusions below bind source meaning, not physical calibration or a live
production history. Pinned`ntp_core.c` SHA256
`ca206410bddc03c56996bba2bddbca2ab01029f9c5b619d4a55025f37d8c4ac0`
and`util.c` SHA256
`c0e80e237ceb9705fb42407ab9dd4dffbb29c711e48ccca30f925d716d0cd9dd`
match the existing S0.1 ledger; S1.2's retained sourcestats/regress hashes also
match. Source anchors below refer to that exact revision.

**Reference and exact representation.** Select the local-exchange midpoint,
following`ntp_core.c:2161–2188`. The genuine receiving owner retains one actual
monotonic`Instant` origin and its clock/mapping identity across observations.
Convert capture endpoints through checked same-origin durations and checked
`u64` nanoseconds; never create an independent origin for each row. Use the
existing lower monotonic capture endpoints`a=send_before.monotonic−origin` and
`b=read_return.monotonic−origin` as deterministic nominal placements, with`a≤b`.
Compute`whole=a+(b−a)/2` and`half=(b−a)%2`, retaining the half-nanosecond bit in
ordering, differences and reports. This avoids overflow even at`a=b=u64::MAX`.
Bracket widths remain distinct: actual wall-read midpoint placement may lie up
to`(send_width+read_width)/2` later. These are not packet-placement error bounds.
Using both bracket centers instead would require quarter-nanosecond precision.
Receive-completion acquisition age remains separate and is never renewed by a fit.

Let`B=10^9`, original phase numerator`n` over`2^33` seconds, signed raw RTT
numerator`d` over`2^32` seconds and local remainders`r1,r4` in`[0,B)`:

```text
A_phase = B*n − r1 − r4     exact nominal phase = A_phase / 2^33 nanoseconds
A_rtt   = B*d + r4 − r1     exact signed raw RTT = A_rtt / 2^32 nanoseconds
```

Preserve these distinct fixed-scale exact values beside the original arithmetic
and remainder association. They are known representation effects, not accuracy
bounds or regression residuals. Current coordinate bounds give`|n|,|d|≤2^97−2`,
so both corrected numerators lie strictly inside`±(2^97*B)` and signed`i128`.
Keep checked operations. Do not multiply a corrected numerator by`B` again.
Any later integer-nanosecond projection divides this combined numerator directly,
uses nearest/ties-even and retains its signed residual; an out-of-`i64` result
rejects only that projection, not the original exact value. The current bare-Q33
rounding API cannot perform this corrected operation.

**Source-derived quality terms.** Freeze the basic-client calculation below in
seconds, from`ntp_core.c:2161–2199`, `util.c:651–654,908–920` and
`sourcestats.c:528–550,627–642`. Define`L` and`R` from corrected nominal local and
peer intervals; preserve signed`raw_rtt=L−R`. Local quantum`c` is finite/positive;
capture errors are finite/nonnegative; the prior same-source frequency interval
is finite/ordered and bound to the applicable generations.

```text
p = 2^clamp(raw_header_precision, −31, 31)
P = c + p
D = max(abs(raw_rtt), P)
K = (prior_frequency_hi − prior_frequency_lo) / 2
E = max(P, tx_capture_error, rx_capture_error) + K*abs(L)
RD = advertised_root_delay + D
RE = advertised_root_dispersion + E
peer_distance = D/2 + E
root_distance = RD/2 + RE
```

Check each evaluated operation for supported finite output. Both advertised root
fields are unsigned16.16 in the selected Chrony conversion and
[RFC5905 §6](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc5905.html#section-6); do not insert
a signed reinterpretation. Only`peer_distance` enters within-source weighting;
changing only root claims must not change those divisors. Keep the observation's
historical quantum distinct from the current quantum frozen for a weighting call.
`K` is an uncertainty half-width around an estimated relative frequency, not a
bound on absolute relative rate. Unavailable prior evidence cannot silently become
zero skew. Neither this formula nor upstream's startup assumption calibrates
Windows capture accuracy or bounds path asymmetry.

Adopt no source constant as a new product threshold. The policy owner must supply
validated maximum advertised distance, delay and server-processing interval;
source predicates include strict advertised-distance comparison and inclusive
`P≤maximum_delay`, `D−E≤maximum_delay`. Delay aging/relearning remains its named
owner. Genuine scale/date/calendar, clock continuity, capture/mapping/frequency/
asymmetry assumptions and their evidence are required before production admission.
A finite numeric quality result is not that admission.

**Frozen quality population during suffix selection.** The pinned source resolves
this seam: derive divisors once over current fitted-population indices`0..n`,
excluding older runs-only indices`−m..0`. Candidate suffix fits and final statistics
reuse that exact vector. Runs decisions may inspect older unweighted residuals;
no candidate recomputes a median. Only after complete success does pruning change
counts; the report retains the pre-prune derivation-window association. A later
independent evaluation derives fresh weights from its then-current population.
Capacity pruning before a new arrival is a different transition. Sources:
`sourcestats.c:270–309,521–597` and`regress.c:249–312`. Do not invent a refit-until-
stable loop. Existing bounded suffix/runs machinery supplies adaptation evidence;
its fixture scalar/normalization contract still needs a real production kernel.

The live source owner must retain history privately, consume a correlated
completion once and stage exact rows/context, one weight vector, bounded suffix
selection and same-input statistics before one state commit. A numeric-ID tuple
can establish equality, never genuine source/clock provenance. Core representation
construction must not mint authenticated/physically validated evidence. Rejection
preserves all prior statistical state and cannot renew freshness; an expiry failure
must still expose true age/unusable status. The current fixture types cannot supply
corrected phase, half-nanosecond references, divisors above16 or subnormal weights.
Production conditioning/expiry policy and that kernel remain explicit prerequisites.

Independent accepted/rejected controls now include: the existing oscillator slope
case; exact midpoint10.5ns and`u64::MAX` neighbor; bare-zero phase with100ns local
remainders giving`−970175/8388608 ns`; negative raw RTT and its positive neighbor
with equal adjusted delay; exponent pairs`−128/−31` and`31/127`; root claims changing
without changing peer weights; one fixed derivation vector across suffix candidates;
missing/stale physical/context evidence; and actual atomic refusal at every stage.
These specification facts are not executed production-receiver tests.

#### Next bounded bridge: common-run reference and exact anchoring — 6 November 2026

**Contract draft; dependent implementation held** until the
[rational-seconds child](s1.2-regression-readiness.md#exact-rational-seconds-normalization--6-november-2026)
completes its selected critical-owner gates. This card refines the reference and
exact-representation obligations above under REQ-8, BND-1 and
CHECK-6/7/8/11/14/25/27, architecture §§6/7. Engine owns actual acquisition,
run/request lifetime and publication; `time-domain` owns the already selected
shared rational-seconds conversion. Core's production receiver, conditioning,
quality model and genuine date/scale/mapping admission remain their existing
dependent work. This is one step toward that receiver, not a substitute for it.

**Observed source chain on `ce4efa5`.** `execute` captures `started: Instant`
before constructing its private runtime, then passes it through `run_owned` to
`PreparedPilot::run_inner`. The latter derives the deadline from that instant;
the run origin is not retained in `TransportFacts`. `LiveSlot`, `StagedReply`
and `TransportFacts` currently share a different `Arc<()>` for each actual
prepared socket/request. `stage_reply` copies this identity and captures;
`commit_reply` checks request identity, endpoints, profile and pending state
after the processing owner's fresh control/deadline checks. Keep this chain.
Neither equal endpoints, equal `Instant` values nor the per-request `Arc`
establishes a shared execution chart.

The next implementation has these bounded contracts:

1. Create exactly one private `Arc<RunClockContext>` from the real `started`
   argument in `run_inner`; retain its origin and propagate clones through both
   `LiveSlot`s, each staged reply and each terminal ordinary `TransportFacts`.
   No public constructor, caller-supplied origin, process-global registry or
   inferred numeric identifier may mint this context. At terminal commit check
   its pointer identity alongside the existing request/socket, endpoint, profile,
   pending-state and fresh control/deadline guards. Add no await between those
   guards and publication. Preserve consume-once completion and existing refusal
   behavior. Budget one small shared context allocation per run; retaining it
   retains no native socket, runtime or task. It identifies this experimental
   execution chart, not a qualified production clock/mapping generation.
2. Add a fallible immutable experimental relative view, reached from actual
   terminal facts through `relative_to(&anchor)` or an equivalent checked API.
   Require both facts' successful conditional measurements and identical real
   run-context identity. Preserve both original facts, profiles and distinct
   request/local/peer identities by borrowing or immutable ownership; choosing an
   anchor must not merge logical sources, replace either acquisition time or
   change either measurement's original refusal. A cross-run pair rejects even
   if every numeric capture and endpoint happens to compare equal.
3. For each row use the already selected lower captures
   `send_before.monotonic()` and `read_return.monotonic()`.
   Explicitly require send and read at or after the origin and read at or after
   send before calculating checked durations since that origin. Do not rely on
   `checked_duration_since` alone: the Windows implementation can clamp a tiny
   negative difference to zero. Convert admitted durations to checked `u64`
   nanoseconds `a,b`, rejecting unrepresentable endpoints. Derive
   `whole=a+(b-a)/2` and `half=(b-a)%2` with checked operations.
   Retain the exact half-nanosecond reference and its capture association; do
   not sum endpoints, floor an odd midpoint or relabel receipt/processing time.
   Bracket widths and physical placement uncertainty remain separate.
4. Define both differences as **self minus anchor**; positive phase therefore
   means the row's nominal phase exceeds its anchor's, while positive reference
   means its reference is later. Anchor before floating conversion. Compare the two retained corrected
   `NominalPhaseNs::numerator()` values, take their `i128::abs_diff` as `u128`
   magnitude and retain the resulting sign. Convert that exact difference once
   with seconds denominator `8_589_934_592_000_000_000`; never subtract separately
   rounded absolute phases or require the difference to fit `i128`. For temporal
   differences, encode each exact reference as `2*whole+half` in `u128`, compare
   and subtract there, then use seconds denominator `2_000_000_000`. Carry both
   original exact quantities and each arithmetic-only rounding bound. These
   calculations supply no physical accuracy or statistical-weight claim.
5. Publish the complete view only after all context, chronology, arithmetic and
   mandatory transformed-output checks succeed. Use private fields, checked
   construction and fixed typed refusals; expose no unchecked construction or
   mutation bypass. Conversion reads no ambient clock and changes no facts,
   freshness, report, history or owner state. Preserve the current twenty-field
   diagnostic schema and existing engine-only runtime/dependency direction.

**Independent acceptance controls.** Literal midpoint `[10,11]` must retain
`10 + 1/2 ns`; `[u64::MAX,u64::MAX]` must retain `u64::MAX` without overflowing
an endpoint sum. Include equal/adjacent endpoints, before-origin and reversed
captures, the checked `u64` duration boundary and its first rejected neighbor.
For a directional literal, row phase2 and anchor phase1 yield a positive
one-numerator-tick difference; row reference11ns and anchor10ns yield positive1ns.
For the private numeric helper use large admitted numerators around `2^120+1`
whose separate binary64 projections collapse while an exact one-tick difference
survives. Opposite-sign numerically admitted values such as `±(2^126+1)` exercise
a difference larger than `i128::MAX` that still fits `u128`. Construct these
through the checked nominal-arithmetic boundary with independent valid inputs;
they are not genuine captured measurements under one unique common-era window.
Actual wall-capture remainders are multiples of512, so a one-numerator-tick
example is also a raw numeric-helper control, not an asserted real-capture case.
Keep the real two-peer acquisition control separate. Self-reference produces canonical
positive zero; swapping row and anchor preserves magnitudes and reverses nonzero
signs. Recompute expected bits/bounds independently with exact rational/dyadic
midpoints, using the public converter's previously selected error semantics.

Actual controlled two-peer UDP execution must return two terminal facts sharing
the genuine run context while `same_exchange` remains false. Exercise the public
relative consumer on that report; a separate run rejects as an anchor. Private
direct-handler controls challenge substituted run/request identity, mismatched
profile/endpoints, stopped/stale control, deadline expiry and duplicate completion
at the final commit. Observe unchanged terminal facts and no second publication,
with a current healthy neighbor. External legal-use and compile-fail consumers
challenge raw construction, field mutation and unchecked conversion. Synthetic
integer/`Instant` fixtures support arithmetic or refusal checks; they do not
replace the actual origin-to-report integration control.

**Exit and finite execution budget.** Before code, independently review this
contract against the actual source and reconcile the active rational gate's
terminal receipt. One root owner runs the existing admitted Linux sandbox recipes:
one intentional feature-omission RED, one coherent ordinary/optimized/doctest
GREEN cohort, and at most two repairs/retries justified by actual failures.
Freeze after formatting; enumerate each changed critical owner and complete its
configured 90% line/95% viable mutation gates, generation-gap controls and complete
survivor accounting. Retain the existing 512-variant per-file cap, ten mutation
workers with one inner job and at most six ordinary build jobs; schedule Cargo
campaigns serially. Check fresh disk capacity and stop reserve first, retain all
failed/partial attempts and reconcile the cumulative budget before any extension.
Windows-target checks remain compilation only. No native Windows, formal solver,
fuzz/storage experiment, dependency expansion or new runtime framework follows.

The pilot still returns one observation per peer. Its cross-peer relative view
cannot establish a same-source history, frequency fit, physical calibration,
production `ValidatedSample`, authenticated eligibility, selection vote or clock
authority. After this bridge, continue the existing production normalization,
conditioning and checked-receiver chain with its original source/provenance
requirements and holds; do not call this bridge full S2.1, P1 or product completion.

**Commit-order refinement from current source review.** While touching the final
commit owner for run-context identity, explicitly reject
`processing < candidate.read.mono_after` before retaining the checked-duration
fallback. Increment rejection once and preserve the pending slot and its prior
history, matching the existing equal/predecessor regression. The current helper
relies solely on `checked_duration_since`; selected Rust1.99.1 Windows source
returns `Some(0)` when a negative interval is within the QPC epsilon. Its `Ord`
comparison still orders those instants. Therefore a one-nanosecond predecessor
can pass the duration check where the QPC epsilon is at least one nanosecond,
contradicting the helper's rejection contract. The existing test
`commit_processing_accepts_equal_read_and_rejects_its_predecessor` already supplies
that predecessor and the legal equality neighbor. Preserve it and explicitly
check their ordering; a later successor must retain a positive age.

This is a source-established conditional counterexample, not an observed Windows

### Pure conditioned-window contract — N1 source preparation

**Revision N1b, 7 November 2026; independently reviewed for source implementation.** Engine and S1.2
jointly own this finite numerical-preparation child under REQ-8/BND-1 and
CHECK-6/7/8/11/14/25/27. Production admission/history stays open. N1 adds no
engine-to-Core Cargo edge, live source, fit, statistics, expiry policy or state
transition. No formal or Windows execution is part of this child.

**Direct design.** Add one pure Core entry point, provisionally
`prepare_conditioned_batch`, and a private prepared result. Keep `FixtureObservation`,
`FitPoint`, `FitInput` and fixture history unchanged. They lose corrected phase/
half ticks and reject subnormal weights. Common divisor scaling cannot fit a
ratio above256 into[1/16,16]. Reuse the already adopted `time-domain` rational
converter and `WindowVarianceDivisors`; add no dependency or arbitrary weight cap.

**Declared input and association.** A private, fallibly constructed numeric row
retains an explicitly asserted source/configuration/association/clock/mapping
context, nonzero asserted observation key, exact reference `(whole_ns:u64,half:bool)`,
corrected phase `A:i128` over `D=8_589_934_592_000_000_000` seconds, and positive
finite peer-distance seconds. Reject `(u64::MAX,true)`, impossible for the
selected u64-nanosecond capture chart. Admit the full i128 numeric phase domain
without claiming all values came from the engine. Mandatory applicable Garde
rules and shared typed-error predicates own construction; every primitive or
already checked field needs an explicit invariant owner, not optional validation.
These are declared associations, not physical, authenticated or current authority.

The preparation call accepts one expected declared context, an immutable original
row slice and raw current precision seconds. Its result borrows that original
slice and preserves index association; it accepts no separate divisor vector.
A later engine owner must retain actual completion identity/acquisition metadata
alongside these rows and recheck current context. This numeric operation neither
reconstructs nor renews acquisition age. Numeric keys cannot prevent a producer
from relabelling a completion; that genuine replay/publication owner is outside N1.

**Admission/failure order.** Bound count to0..64 before reading rows; admit
positive finite current precision, including positive subnormals; compare every
row context with the expected context; reject duplicate keys in original order;
then reject the first non-increasing exact reference. Order uses the full65-bit
half-tick `h=2*whole_ns+u128::from(half)`, not floating or floored nanoseconds.
Next derive whole-window divisors, then convert coordinates in original order,
condition and check transformed outputs. Row construction has its own earlier
field errors; the batch never accepts an unchecked raw row. Errors contain only
bounded count/index and fixed stage/cause, with nested typed causes. Never print
raw timestamps/distances or Garde reports.

**One population and insufficient counts.** Copy original-order distances into
fixed scratch and invoke existing whole-window derivation exactly once with the
explicit current precision. Copy its complete checked divisors into privately
associated bounded storage, unchanged and in original order; avoid a
self-referential owner borrowing its own scratch. Retain precision, distance bits
and pre-prune population association. A later checked suffix view slices that
same vector, never rederives medians or accepts another equal-length evaluation.
Runs-only rows stay outside this population. No cap, replacement or row dropping.

Counts0/1/2 return a distinct admitted insufficient result retaining original
rows, context/count and exact newest reference/phase anchors when present. Empty
input has no anchor/vector or derivation; explicit precision is still admitted.
One/two rows pass all applicable membership/order/quality checks and retain the
derived vector, but perform no coordinate conversion or geometry. A real quality
arithmetic failure stays an error, never insufficient success. Insufficient
variants expose no fit-ready batch, coefficient or readiness flag.

**Exact subtraction and conditioning for3..64 rows.** Anchor reference and phase
at the newest row. Form exact signed `x_i=(h_i-h_newest)/2_000_000_000` seconds
and `y_i=(A_i-A_newest)/D` seconds before floating conversion. Use sign plus
unsigned `abs_diff` so the difference of i128 extrema fits u128. Convert each
difference once with `RoundedRationalSeconds`, retaining its exact tuple and
arithmetic-only bound. Do not convert absolute phases then subtract, discard half
ticks or project through integer nanoseconds/bare Q33.

Choose `e_t` as the smallest integer with exact oldest-reference span<=`2^e_t`
seconds; choose `e_p` from the largest exact absolute anchored phase similarly.
All-zero phase uses `e_p=0`. Compare integer ratios with powers of two, not
floating logarithms. Three strictly increasing half-tick rows imply at least1ns
span, giving `e_t in[-29,35]`; `e_p in[-62,66]` covers the full raw phase domain.
Compute a nonzero ratio's ceiling exponent without an overflowing shifted
denominator: for numerator m>=denominator d, use
`floor_log2(m/d)+(m%d!=0 || m/d is not a power of two)`; for0<m<d, use
`-floor_log2(d/m)`. All divisions here are exact integer quotient/remainder.
Never rely on `checked_shl` to detect discarded high bits. Separately multiply
each once-rounded x/y and its bound by
exact binary powers `2^-e_t`/`2^-e_p`. Coordinates and nonzero bounds remain
normal in this domain, making this scaling exact; reject any contradiction.
Retain exponents, exact anchors/differences, unscaled rounded values and scaled
bounds. Prepared z/v are finite in[-1,0]/[-1,1]; newest coordinates are positive
zero. In addition to exact order, enforce strictly increasing rounded z. If
distinct exact references alias, return whole-window `RoundedReferenceCollision`;
never merge/drop rows, change reference or widen tolerance. Half-tick references
`(0,1,2^64)` provide a concrete exact-order/rounded-collision case.

**Checked receiver and publication.** Only complete preparation can construct
`PreparedConditionedBatch`, after all rows, one weight vector, anchors,
conversions, scales and mandatory transformed-output rules pass. Private fields,
immutable bounded point/original-row getters
must prevent `Default`, unchecked `From`, deserialization, mutation or independent
context relabelling. Index getters return `None` for every index at or above the
retained count, including `usize::MAX`, without effects or panic. Original rows
remain borrowed unchanged. Public suffix construction/views and their specific
tests are deferred entirely to N2; N1 exposes no suffix API or new conditioning
of a subset. This is the new numeric input owner, not a fixture `FitInput`.
N1 does not execute or qualify its future fitting consumer. Work/storage are
bounded by64 rows and fixed scratch, with no successful heap allocation, I/O,
ambient reads, logging or caller-state mutation. Rejection may allocate bounded
temporary Garde reports; it preserves input bits and returns no partial payload.

**Separate N2 fit prerequisite.** Before fitting, freeze and qualify the new
primitive/reduction/geometry/statistics domain, operation order and independently
justified accuracy/error criteria. Finite divisors>=1 have legal positive
subnormal reciprocals; fixture scalar rejection cannot categorically reject those
raw weights. A checked unsupported product, nonzero-to-zero underflow, overflow
or unresolved geometry may refuse the whole fit, with no fallback or fake zero
slope. Preserve exact phase anchor: mathematical normalized outputs `(a',b',S')`
map to anchored phase `A_newest/D+2^e_p*a'`, rate `2^(e_p-e_t)*b'` and weighted
SSE `2^(2*e_p)*S'`. Actual reconstruction needs its own checked rounding/range/error
contract. Finite normalized outputs/input-conversion bounds prove no final fit
accuracy or physical capture/path uncertainty. N2 remains held, not silently
implemented through old fixture primitives.

**Acceptance and finite exit.** Observe actual original rows, exact anchors and
differences, exponent choices, scaled values/bounds and unchanged divisors.
Challenge0/1/2/3/64/65 counts; invalid precision and positive-subnormal neighbors;
context/key/order bypasses and adjacent half ticks; rounded collision; full phase
extrema; zero/constant phases; uncapped divisors/subnormal-reciprocal vectors;
late failure with unchanged input bits. External legal and compile-fail controls
challenge construction, mutation, defaults/conversions, association escape
and borrowed row mutation. Include deleted-validator failures and legal neighbors.
Require ordinary/optimized tests, doctests, compiler/Clippy/rustdoc, two-target
checks and existing90% executable-line/95% per-file viable-mutation gates for
each new critical owner. Format before freezing; root alone owns graph/runner
registration and Cargo execution. Historical fixture passes grant no N1 credit.

Prospective independent example: x`=(-2,-1,0)s`, y`=(-1,0,3)ms`, distances
`(1,1,1.5)s`, precision`0.25s` derive divisors`(1,1,4)`.
`e_t=1,e_p=-7` yields normalized y`=(-64/125,-48/125,0)` and mathematical
line/SSE`=(-128/1125,32/75,1024/140625)`. Reverse units give intercept`19/9000s`,
rate`1/600`, SSE`1/2250000s²`. Ordinary exact `Fraction` recalculation confirms
these facts and exponent endpoints; it executes no Rust preparation or fit.
Fit expectations belong to N2, not N1 mutation/coverage credit.

N1's exit is a qualified pure prepared window and associated insufficient results.
It leaves N2, genuine provenance/physical/date/scale evidence, history/expiry,
current publication, actual integration and native/fuzz/storage/UI holds open.
No upstream survey, larger runner project, physical default or proof job is part
of this child.

**N1b implementation boundary.** The public checked numeric row is
`ExactObservationRow`, its five nonzero declared context labels are owned by
`DeclaredObservationContext`, and `prepare_conditioned_batch` returns a closed
prepared/insufficient outcome with private payload fields. Labels/observation
keys assert numerical associations only. Ready points retain a borrow of their
actual original row, exact once-rounded reference/phase deltas, normalized
values/error bounds and the unchanged corresponding divisor. The batch retains
the exact newest row and both scale exponents; insufficient results retain their
appropriate original-row/weight metadata and no normalized points. No external
point constructor or independently attachable weight vector is exposed.

The implementation owner is the new `crates/time-core/src/conditioned_window.rs`
module, with existing-library exports and a dedicated external test. Reuse the
existing positive-finite rule for distance/precision; a narrow `pub(crate)`
visibility change in `window_variance.rs` is permitted for that purpose and

### N2a concrete contract — conditioned suffix and enclosed affine fit

**Independently reviewed READY, 7 November 2026; source and ordinary checks complete,
final qualification pending.** This
expands the existing N2 entry rather than creating another workstream. Predecessor
is qualified N1b380/`7a7d1b7bc692ba2a5f07b3d8ad795d28d97c9cb0f5f1255d9077ff0bb0457274`;
HEAD remains `c8df999abb2309d11cd4484159671a7110c21fa4` with preserved dirty/staged
work. Owner: existing engine/S1.2 numerical Core, REQ-8/BND-1 and
CHECK-6/7/8/11/14/25/27. No engine graph edge, new dependency, production history,
native Windows, clock effect or formal execution is selected.

**Actual producer and API ownership.** A new `prepare_conditioned_suffix` function
accepts `&'batch PreparedConditionedBatch<'rows>` and raw `start:usize`.
`start>parent.sample_count()`, including `usize::MAX`, returns a typed range error
before subtraction/access. Otherwise `[start,n)` is the complete selected suffix.
Counts0/1/2 return `ConditionedSuffixOutcome::Insufficient` containing an opaque
`InsufficientConditionedSuffix`; counts3..64 return `Ready(ConditionedSuffix)`.
Both retain the actual parent borrow, original start/count and access to its
unchanged full-population metadata, even for an empty suffix. They accept no raw
point, weight, frame, anchor or scale arguments. N1's separate insufficient batch
has no normalized points and is not an input to this API.

Local point/row getters first check `index<suffix_count`, then checked original-
index addition and the actual parent's getter. Out-of-range indices return `None`
without panic/effect. A ready suffix borrows the parent, whose immutable original
rows remain borrowed separately. `fit_conditioned_suffix(&'fit ConditionedSuffix)`
returns a private `ConditionedFit` borrowing that exact ready suffix. Its producer
publishes normalized and lifted estimates together only after every stage passes.
It exposes no unchecked constructor, `Default`, deserialization, mutable access,
independent certificate attachment or legacy fixture conversion. The public
read-only scalar wrappers distinguish normalized estimates, phase-correction
seconds, frequency ratio and weighted seconds-squared. Each exposes computed
point, lower/upper enclosure and absolute arithmetic-error bound, with private
construction. Fit fields identify normalized intercept/rate/weighted SSE and
their separately lifted counterparts; debug/errors redact numeric payloads.

**Exact mathematical target.** Let h be the exact half-nanosecond reference, A the
full corrected-phase numerator, D=`8589934592000000000`, and retain the parent's
newest row n and exact exponents `e_t/e_p`. For every selected original row:

```text
z* = ((h_i-h_n)/2000000000) * 2^(-e_t)
v* = ((A_i-A_n)/D) * 2^(-e_p)
r* = 1/q_i
```

Here q denotes its retained binary64 divisor exactly, not an ideal upstream
quality formula. The target `(a*,b*)` minimizes `sum(r*(v*-a*-b*z*)^2)`;
`S*` is that exact optimal weighted SSE. Exact references are strictly ordered
and weights strictly positive, so a ready suffix has a unique exact line. An
unresolved result is a numerical/enclosure refusal, not proof of exact singularity.
All suffixes use the full parent's existing newest anchors, scales, precision and
unchanged q bits; no median/quality rederivation, common weight scaling, coordinate
rescaling/reanchoring or row dropping occurs in N2a.

**Point domain and deterministic recipe.** Binary64 round-to-nearest/ties-even
with gradual underflow, separate operations and chronological left reductions
is the numerical execution contract. Each primitive admits finite operands and
finite results, including signed subnormals. Division requires a positive stored
denominator. Nonfinite values and multiplication/division of nonzero stored
operands/numerator rounded to zero are typed failures at their actual stage/index.
Addition/subtraction cancellation to zero is legal. Numerical zeros are
canonicalized to positive zero; no nonzero result is clamped. A zero computed
coefficient/SSE does not imply exact-source zero. No FMA/reassociation or fallback
constant/previous result is used. Geometry precedes response arithmetic:

```text
pass 1: compute w_i=RN(1/q_i) once into associated fixed scratch;
        W=RN(W+w_i), Z=RN(Z+RN(w_i*z_i)); mu_z=RN(Z/W)
pass 2: dz=RN(z_i-mu_z), wd=RN(w_i*dz), G=RN(G+RN(wd*dz))
        require point G>0 and the completed exact-target interval G.lower>0
pass 3: V=RN(V+RN(w_i*v_i)); mu_v=RN(V/W)
pass 4: dz=RN(z_i-mu_z), dv=RN(v_i-mu_v), wd=RN(w_i*dz)
        C=RN(C+RN(wd*dv))
        b=RN(C/G); a=RN(mu_v-RN(b*mu_z))
pass 5: t=RN(v_i-a); p=RN(b*z_i); residual=RN(t-p)
        S=RN(S+RN(RN(w_i*residual)*residual))
```

All reductions start at positive zero. The same saved reciprocal is used by
every pass. Geometry refusal occurs before response products. There is no
special constant shortcut: exact constant-phase deltas are zero and pass through
the same admitted geometry and exact-zero identities. The implementation is
five bounded O(n) passes over at most64 points with fixed reciprocal/enclosure
scratch; no recursion, pair table or successful heap allocation. Rejected Garde
reports remain bounded temporary allocations, not logged/returned.

**Enclosure owner and accuracy contract.** New private finite closed intervals
own this domain. Existing fixture `arithmetic`, point constructors and chart
companions reject subnormal points or impose unrelated caps/spans/spread; their
unchanged implementations are not N2's numeric owner. Seed each exact coordinate
from the actual N1 point and conversion bound: zero bound gives the exact
singleton; otherwise outwardly evaluate `[z-e_z,z+e_z]` and `[v-e_v,v+e_v]`.
Reciprocal intervals enclose exact `1/q`, including positive subnormal results.

Endpoint Add/Subtract/Multiply/positive-Divide evaluates the binary64 primitive,
then moves downward/upward with `next_down`/`next_up`. Preserve only explicit
exact identities: adding zero, subtracting equal finite operands or zero,
negating a finite operand, multiplying by zero or one, zero numerator and
division by one/equal positive operands. Normalize zero representation only.
Enclosure underflow widens outward; it is not point underflow and must not use a
`MIN_POSITIVE` normal floor. Reject nonfinite/exhausted finite endpoints. Generic
interval multiplication/division uses all four endpoint combinations; division
requires a strictly positive denominator lower endpoint. A dedicated square has
lower zero when its input straddles zero, otherwise the outward minimum endpoint
square, and the outward maximum endpoint square as upper bound. Mathematical
nonnegative ranges may strengthen enclosure bounds; no computed point is clamped.

Evaluate the exact-real centred equations through this interval owner: sums of
R, R*Z and R*V; positive-margin division for the two means; G=sum(R*square(Z-mu_z)),
C=sum((R*(Z-mu_z))*(V-mu_v)); B=C/G; A=mu_v-B*mu_z; and
S=sum(R*square(V-(A+B*Z))). This interval chain includes N1 conversion, exact
reciprocal, reduction, coefficient and residual/SSE uncertainty. It follows the
same staged geometry-first ownership; intervals are not computed from a claimed
zero-error rounded fit afterward. Unavailable positive G margin returns
`UnresolvedGeometry`; the fixture `1/256` cutoff is not imported.

For each published scalar take the hull of the exact-target interval and actual
computed point. Outwardly bound the maximum distance from that point to either
endpoint. The result guarantees exact-target containment, point containment and
`abs(point-exact_target)<=absolute_error`, including cancellation/underflow in
bound calculations. Nonfinite/error-range exhaustion refuses the whole fit.
This is a certified arithmetic enclosure, not a universal relative-accuracy
threshold or physical/statistical uncertainty. Width remains explicit for later
admission; no product accuracy budget is invented. An exact-source SSE is
nonnegative and at most the suffix count, but a rounded computed SSE is not
silently clamped to that exact-target bound.

**Checked lifting and exact phase anchor.** Keep the actual newest row's A/D
anchor exact and borrowed. Never form an absolute binary64 phase. Lift targets
as relative phase correction `2^e_p*a*`, frequency ratio `2^(e_p-e_t)*b*` and
weighted seconds-squared `2^(2*e_p)*S*`. The exact power factors use checked
binary exponent encoding with exponent ranges[-62,66],[-97,95],[-124,132]
respectively. Checked point multiplication and outward interval multiplication
produce fresh hull/error fields, including lifting roundoff/range failures.
The phase result means exact anchor plus enclosed correction. A late lifting or
validation failure returns no partial normalized/lifted object.

**Enforcement and errors.** Newly admitted suffix indices, raw scalar/interval
representations and final normalized/lifted outputs have shared predicates and
mandatory applicable Garde validation in their sole private producers. Checked
N1 data is reused immutably; a skipped field must name that actual owner. The
producer algorithm owns the mathematical interval relationship; Garde checks
representation and cannot certify arbitrary caller-claimed error. All internal
input/output/error/lifetime invariants name their mandatory owner. Use bounded
original index/count, stage, operation and typed cause, with no raw rows, labels,
coefficients, distances, anchors or validator reports in diagnostics. Rejection
preserves every input bit. No ambient read, I/O, logging, history transition,
sample/tracking authority or clock effect occurs.

**Source and provenance boundary.** Proposed owners are
`crates/time-core/src/conditioned_fit.rs` for suffix/fit/output staging and
`crates/time-core/src/conditioned_fit/arithmetic.rs` for the new interval/point
domain, plus external and private tests. Reuse N1 getters unchanged; its stale
single-line “no suffix/fit” documentation may be corrected without changing its
numeric implementation. Library exports, critical-owner registration and an
additive selected analysis role are coordinator-owned. No Cargo graph change.

#### Receiving transaction reconciliation — 7 November 2026

Current source review inspected clean `e5d9a0112e198ab41a50ee0b0057e06eab68ab84`.
Three fresh-context read-only workers completed distinct identity/publication,
population/atomicity and actual-supplier questions. They ran no Cargo, native,
formal or fuzz jobs. The coordinator reconciled their source findings with
architecture §§6,7.2,7.3,9,10.1 and17.1 and the existing refinement above.
This closes the four engineering choices, not their implementation or the
unavailable production suppliers. It supersedes the earlier unresolved-choice
status, while retaining every physical, date/scale and current-authority hold.

1. **Actual capability and chart.** The authoritative source/association owner
   must establish a private chart and source-context capability **before**
   acquisition, and carry them through preparation, execution and completion.
   A private receiving adapter consumes the genuine report/completion once and
   derives each row itself, retaining actual run/request identity, original
   attempt position, captures and a code-owned row-key association. It accepts
   no caller-paired facts/row or posthoc context-label substitute. Current
   `RequestOwner`/`Completion`/`SeriesReceiver::receive` already bind real
   request/run `Arc` identities; `PreparedSeries` and `RunClockContext` do not
   supply production source/configuration/association/clock/mapping authority.
   Each current `execute_series` creates a fresh chart. Existing independent
   reports therefore cannot be accumulated by equal endpoints, labels or origin
   values. The future accumulated source history requires an actual shared
   pre-acquisition chart or separately justified original-capture mapping.
2. **Transaction and insufficiency.** One terminal completion is one statistical
   transaction; visit report positions in original order. Successful N1 one/two-
   row `Insufficient` collection commits rows without a fit. Third-or-later
   collection commits only after complete N1/N2 and current-authority success.
   Any quality, conversion, geometry, arithmetic or final-guard refusal preserves
   the prior rows/reference/fit/revision and their original ages. Earlier
   successful transactions in the same report remain committed. Refusal never
   restores a consumed request or grants a threshold-seeking retry.
3. **Capacity and parent.** Select oldest-only replacement for the fitted
   population's existing64-row bound. Stage old rows plus the incoming row,
   remove the oldest in scratch when necessary, then derive one complete N1
   parent and evaluate its suffixes without changing anchors or divisor bits.
   Any later refusal leaves the committed64 untouched. This is a reviewed
   engineering selection using the existing atomic-history precedent, not a
   consequence of N1's numeric limit alone. Runs-only auxiliary rows retain
   their separate bounded owner and never enter the fitted-quality population;
   this choice introduces no new auxiliary budget, age threshold or pruning
   policy. Do not search smaller populations until a fit succeeds.
4. **Publication.** Keep N2's borrowed fit operation-local. Under the canonical
   receiver's exclusive turn, check the exact staged history/revision and actual
   source/configuration/association/clock/mapping ownership, quality/precision
   and scale/calendar revisions, stop/invalidation and original age of every
   retained row. Use trusted monotonic time, reject backwards time and checked
   age/revision overflow, and keep current freshness policy distinct from the
   historical transport deadline. Commit rows, associations, same-fit diagnostics
   and next revision together without an await, callback or unlock after the
   final guard. A failed replacement cannot hide an old fit's genuine expiry.

These choices preserve the selected source-statistics algorithm and rollback
rules; they select no weaker trust, new physical limits or source-vote policy.
No additional David decision is needed for this mechanism. Genuine production
date/scale evidence, calibrated quality assumptions, freshness/limit policy or a
changed recovery/security rule still belongs to its existing authority and
OD-04 when a material unresolved choice becomes dependent.

**Next actual dependency — Q1 conditional source quality.** Implement the missing
engine-local calculation from the already frozen basic-client formula, before
attempting the production receiver. Its actual path is
`SeriesReport::attempts()` → `SeriesAttempt::report()` → `SlotReport::facts()` →
the same borrowed `TransportFacts` header and successful conditional measurement.
The private result must retain that facts association and immutable checked
assumptions; accepting copied headers/measurements or arbitrary label tuples
would sever it. Q1 adds no Core edge, rows, history, vote, new current-age reading
or eligibility. The result is **computed nominal quality conditional on declared
numeric assumptions**, not calibrated production uncertainty or admission.

Freeze Q1's numerical contract before implementation:

- Mandatory explicit inputs are positive finite historical local quantum `c`,
  nonnegative finite TX/RX capture allowances, finite ordered prior-frequency
  endpoints, and positive finite maximum advertised distance, delay and absolute
  server-processing interval. Private fallible construction invokes shared
  garde rules; there is no default, builder/deserializer bypass or substitution
  for missing evidence. Checked numeric assumptions do not assert their physical
  truth, common-source provenance or present validity. Historical `c` does not
  supply N1's separate current precision.
- With `B=10^9`, compute checked signed exact differences first:
  `L=[B*(T4_ticks−T1_ticks)+r4−r1]/(B*2^32)` and
  `R=(T3_ticks−T2_ticks)/2^32`. Independently use the actual corrected nominal
  RTT numerator over `B*2^32`; never subtract rounded `L` and `R`, use the bare
  Q32 RTT, monotonic elapsed or subtract independently rounded absolute times.
  Use the existing once-rounded rational-seconds owner and retain each exact
  tuple and representation-error bound separately.
- Evaluate the existing `p/P/D/K/E/RD/RE/peer_distance/root_distance` formula in
  its stated order with separate binary64 operations. Root words are unsigned
  16.16; precision clamps to[-31,31]. Check every intermediate for finiteness
  before `max` or a policy comparison. Output distances/dispersion are positive;
  other legal zero results are canonical positive zero. Positive subnormals and
  gradual underflow, including nonzero frequency half-width or product rounded
  to zero, follow this **computed-value** contract; retain original assumptions
  so no physical exact-zero claim follows. No FMA/reassociation, total arithmetic
  enclosure or statistical-confidence promise is introduced.
- Compare computed advertised `root_delay/2+root_dispersion` **strictly below**
  its explicit maximum; compare `P`, `D−E` and `abs(R)` **inclusively** to their
  respective delay/delay/server-processing maxima. Preserve signed `R` and raw
  RTT. The pinned basic-client source uses absolute response time; Q1 introduces
  neither a new `T3>=T2` rule nor blanket negative-RTT rejection. A failed
  measurement remains its original typed refusal; invalid assumptions, checked
  arithmetic/nonfinite output and each policy refusal remain distinct bounded
  typed failures with no state mutation or payload-bearing diagnostic.

##### Persistent session API and lifecycle — 8 November 2026

**Reviewed implementation contract; source authoring starts below.** Reuse the
actual checked `SeriesConfig`, `prepare_series` and consuming `execute_series`.
Create an opaque `AcquisitionSession` from an owned immutable configuration and
an engine-created chart before any preparation. Add no dependency, runtime,
mutex, queue, external retire channel or production authority. Existing one-shot
APIs and `TransportFacts::relative_to` retain their contracts.

Selected API shape:

```text
AcquisitionSession::new(SeriesConfig) -> AcquisitionSession
AcquisitionSession::prepare(&mut self)
    -> Result<(PreparedAcquisition<'_>, ControlHandle), AcquisitionError>
PreparedAcquisition::execute(self, &ControlSchedule)
    -> Result<AcquisitionReport, AcquisitionError>
AcquisitionSession::retire(&mut self)
AcquisitionReport::series(&self) -> &SeriesReport
AcquisitionReport::chart_origin(&self) -> Instant
AcquisitionReport::same_session(&self, &Self) -> bool
```

The prepared guard holds an exclusive mutable session borrow and the actual
private prepared plan; execution retains that borrow through the existing
driver's timer/runtime cleanup. Only that operation can construct a report
wrapper, from its genuine returned series and the chart captured before its
preparation. No public operation accepts a caller-paired report, facts/context
tuple or chart. The immutable report retains metadata only; matching provenance
is not current authority. The session retains no report collection. Private
identity, checked configuration reuse and owning construction enforce these
invariants; no new raw scalar admission or second validation rule is introduced.

| State/action | Required result and effect |
| --- | --- |
| Idle, successful prepare | One Prepared guard; no socket/runtime; existing entropy/token admission is unchanged. |
| Idle, failed prepare | Original typed driver refusal; remain Idle; no hidden retry. |
| Prepared guard dropped unused | Drop the actual prepared resources before returning the session to Idle. |
| Prepared guard deliberately forgotten | Remain Busy; another prepare refuses before entropy/native work. |
| Execute consumes guard | Transition to Executing before invoking the driver; no second preparation or quiescent retirement is expressible while its exclusive borrow is held. |
| Driver returns clean Completed, Stopped or SeriesDeadline | Return the original report with pre-acquisition chart metadata; return to Idle only after cleanup. |
| Driver returns Stale/Failure or any primary/cleanup failure | Retain the original report and retire the session. |
| Driver returns an outer error or encounters a structural contradiction | Retire the session; preserve the actual typed error; never reopen capacity as if execution completed normally. |
| Unexpected unwind | Drop marks the session Retired and the original unwind propagates; add no production panic catcher or fabricated typed failure. |
| Quiescent retire | Idempotently select terminal Retired, including a forgotten-plan Busy state; all later preparations refuse. |
| Configuration/association replacement | Retire/drop the old quiescent owner and create a fresh session/chart; no in-place mutation or old-handle retargeting. |

Retirement is explicitly quiescent. Active interruption uses the original plan's
`ControlHandle`; the caller waits for actual execution/cleanup before retirement
or replacement. A stop request is delivery, not completion. Normal report
classification preserves the qualified historical-transfer/fresh-summary cutoff;
no wrapper can erase a historical completion or renew its age. Unknown Windows
native completion remains unqualified under the existing execution hold.

pre-acquisition chart with distinct run/request identities; equal-endpoint
foreign sessions; old-handle isolation; plan drop/forget; entered-runtime
preparation/execution failures; explicit retirement and stale/failure retirement;
private construction/substitution/mutation/duplicate-consumption and simultaneous
prepare/retire bypasses through external compile-fail plus legal-use controls.
Use bounded native loopback fixtures and direct private transition controls where
an actual backend failure cannot be forced. Such synthetic transition controls
must not be called observed native failures. Requirements remain REQ-8/BND-1 and
CHECK-6/7/8/11/14/15/23/25/26/27 within this controlled Linux slice.

`AcquisitionReport::reference_at(position: u8)` returns a private immutable
`AcquisitionReference<'_>` or `AcquisitionReferenceRefusal`, in this exact order:

1. Checked original-position lookup; reject outside 0..3 as `AttemptPosition`.
2. Obtain that original attempt's terminal report and facts; return
   `FactsUnavailable` for unstarted or fact-free outcomes. Their original causes
   remain available through the unmodified series report.
3. Require those facts' original successful `ExperimentalMeasurement`; preserve
   the exact `MeasurementRefusal` as `Measurement(reason)` before reference work.
4. Call the existing checked reference owner with the report's private session
   origin and its own lower send/read captures; preserve `Reference(reason)`.
   The reachable nested reasons are before-origin, order, range, arithmetic and
   output invariant; this path does not compare runs or recalculate measurements.
