##### W11-W01 finite capture/mapping supplier contract — 8 November 2026

The next source-contract checkpoint is settled under CHECK-6/7/8/9/10/11/15/21/
25/27, with acquisition/platform and the existing receiving owner. This selects
no new platform API, dependency, affirmative mapping provider or execution
permission. Rust 1.99.1 commit `58cf8f9c35b355199727627d07ae34a4e2846d67` and
the selected Tokio/Mio source were inspected locally and against pinned official
upstream source; vendor files match checksums. The actual producer chain is:

`Instant/SystemTime/Instant` wall-read brackets → successful synchronous send-call
or selected receive-return capture → conditional measurement → serialized final
control/deadline commit → immutable facts → consume-once transfer → driver cleanup
→ session classification. The private run/session identities preserve historical
association, not mapping authority. Tokio readiness ultimately invokes the
synchronous Mio/std payload operations; this is not complete IOCP/AFD teardown
qualification.

| Claim | Actual supplier and source limit | Required receiving behavior |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Capture placement and association | Original software brackets, actual socket/request/endpoint/header association. Send success does not establish delivery; queued ingress can precede the receive call. | Retain exact original readings and placement category. No invented wire-error interval or renewed age. |
| Clock representation | Pinned Windows std uses QPC/QPF for Instant and GetSystemTimePreciseAsFileTime for SystemTime. Numeric resolution is not physical precision or external UTC accuracy. | Preserve distinct domains and checked conversions; reject explicit order violations before Windows' small-negative subtraction tolerance can mask them. |
| Clock/mapping identity, validity and finite errors | No current implemented issuer. Software consistency checks only challenge the explicit experimental declaration. | Production mapping remains unavailable until an actual owner supplies private identity/revision, scale, validity covering the whole exchange and justified error terms. Missing terms never become zero. |
| Configuration/association/calendar/source context | Current owners must issue their own identities and revisions; the pilot watch epoch and caller labels are not those authorities. | Bind before acquisition; replacement invalidates old work despite equal endpoints or labels. Independently require date/scale/source and calibration suppliers. |
| Clock/power change and invalidation | Actual broker acknowledgements and qualified platform events are future suppliers. HandlerEx time-change notification is retrospective, not an atomic mapping lease. | Withdraw issuance first, advance checked revision, and retire crossing/pending work. Unknown event coverage, unsupported backend and revision exhaustion remain unavailable. |
| Final publication and late work | The actual receiving/native-operation owners, not ControlHandle delivery alone. | Recheck issuer/revision, full validity, current context, control, deadline and original age at one serialized admission commit. Late completion is charged once and retains native custody without publishing current admission; no await/callback/unlock splits guard and commit. |

The source-contract acceptance matrix requires a genuine valid neighboring
issuance, each absent supplier, stale/replaced identity, invalidation immediately
before publication, late completion, revision exhaustion and retained facts/ages
after retirement. Synthetic issuers/backend controls establish only their stated
scope. No detached `MappingValid` model or positive capability is implemented
merely to exercise that matrix. Actual provider/native implementation is held
until its suppliers/admission exist; existing history/Core integration stays held.

[pinned Tokio UDP](https://github.com/tokio-rs/tokio/blob/b53b2001ed2ccfd9b87e32a0c6da1a1eebdc80b8/tokio/src/net/udp.rs),
[pinned Mio UDP](https://github.com/tokio-rs/mio/blob/79d1417c94e3a45c8bdd796e5422e22e8c488086/src/net/udp.rs),
[QPC semantics](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/sysinfo/acquiring-high-resolution-time-stamps),
[precise wall reads](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/sysinfoapi/nf-sysinfoapi-getsystemtimepreciseasfiletime),
[sendto](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winsock2/nf-winsock2-sendto),
[recvfrom](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winsock2/nf-winsock2-recvfrom),
[HandlerEx](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winsvc/nc-winsvc-lphandler_function_ex).

source evidence; no Windows command, installation or native qualification ran.

##### Within-series observed-coherence veto — implementation contract

Independent ownership and test review select one useful portable child of that
contract. `ExperimentalProfile` explicitly declares coherence over **one bounded
run**. Assess all retained captures in one genuine returned `SeriesReport`; do
not extend its allowance across separately requested series or retain a rolling
wall baseline. No new physical threshold or owner policy decision is introduced.

Add a privately derived report field and immutable `coherence()` getter using
`SeriesCoherence::{ObservedCompatible, ObservedInconsistent, Unavailable(reason)}`
and `CoherenceUnavailable::{NoProfile, NoFacts, Capture(MeasurementRefusal)}`.
Only the real consuming driver-return path attaches this assessment. Reachable
checked capture causes are `CaptureOrder` and `Arithmetic`; no raw payload or
validation report escapes. Original series/end/failure fields, measurements,
refusals, ages and historical `reference_at` behavior remain unchanged.

Assessment order is fixed: no original profile yields NoProfile first; otherwise
visit original attempts and each retained facts object's send-before/send-after/
read-return triplet, skipping absent facts without inventing captures. At most
nine captures exist. With none, return NoFacts. Keep the first retained send-before
as fixed baseline. Check every bracket and the complete retained sequence before
subtraction (`mono <= mono_after`, previous-after <= next-before), then complete
all checked wall conversions and baseline residuals in original order. Any
checked failure wins over an earlier threshold excess. Only after full success,
maximum residual strictly greater than the existing declared limit is
ObservedInconsistent; equality is ObservedCompatible. Compatibility establishes
neither all-pairs agreement, missing-capture coverage, continuity nor finite
physical error. Original measurement success is not required for assessment.

After actual driver/cleanup return, inconsistency or checked-capture failure
adds a terminal retirement veto. Compatible/NoProfile/NoFacts add no veto, but
cannot override existing Stale/Failure/primary/cleanup retirement. No historical
driver completion is relabelled or said to have been prevented. No clock read,
capture queue, affirmative mapping capability or automatic retry is introduced.

Controls: zero/one/three retained facts; holes and all nine captures; first-baseline
drift ramp; exact-limit/just-over neighbors; own/crossed ordering, touching equality,
conversion/arithmetic failure, and excess followed by later invalid input;
assessment × existing completion causes; genuine healthy execution and DateScope
refusal with compatible local captures; NoProfile/NoFacts precedence; metadata
privacy and original-object/age preservation. Synthetic assessment/classifier
controls remain distinct from actual contradictory-clock execution, which is
unobserved without an admitted clock seam/experiment. Do not mutate the host clock.

Before reuse, repair `coherence_residual`'s raw-helper cross-bracket guard. Current
measure callers already perform explicit whole-exchange ordering, so this is a
source-derived helper contract gap, not a demonstrated current product defect.
Pinned Windows subtraction may otherwise hide a small negative lower interval.
An isolated source-linked Windows-epsilon subtraction surrogate can provide
behavioral RED/GREEN, labelled separately from native Windows execution. Preserve
direct Linux controls and all existing raw math cases. Formal execution remains
post-V1. Format before freezing, then complete affected ordinary/contracts and
per-file 90%/95% qualification; source presence alone closes no gate.

#### N2a complete first campaign and strengthened candidate

The complete first campaign is retained as incomplete, despite raw percentages
above 95%. Fit: 143 generated, 83 caught, 57 compile/lint-unviable, three survivors;
arithmetic: 161 generated, 96 caught, 64 unviable, one survivor. Both baselines execute

Two are genuine contract-test gaps. Changing `original_index`'s `<` to `<=`
admits a one-past local index into its actual diagnostic-context constructor,
even though downstream public getters mask it. Changing interval subtraction's

### N3a finite same-source acquisition and completion receiver contract

**Independently reviewed READY for finite source implementation, 7 November
2026.** This is the first admitted acquisition/receiver child after N2, within
the existing observation-to-Core chain. Production sample/history admission and
all native execution holds remain separate.
Owner: engine/protocol/QA, with S1.2 retaining later numerical/history admission.
Requirements: REQ-8/BND-1 and CHECK-6/7/8/11/14/25/27. Environment: admitted Linux,
controlled loopback UDP only; no Windows, formal, public network or clock effects.

**Observable source exit.** One engine-owned series makes exactly three planned
sequential attempts to one explicitly configured loopback endpoint and moves
each terminal completion once into bounded private receiving state. Three is a
fixed attempt count, not a target of three successful measurements or permission
to retry failed requests. The report distinguishes completed transport facts,
nominal measurement refusal/unavailability and attempts not started because of
terminal control/deadline/setup failure. Statistical admission is explicitly
unavailable and admitted/eligible sample count remains zero.

**Actual owners and API.** Add a small `series` owner in `nevrotick-engine`, with
minimal `lib.rs` orchestration/reuse changes and focused private/public tests.
Reuse actual `LiveSlot`, staging/commit/retirement, `measurement.rs` and
`relative.rs`; add no Cargo dependency, generic runtime framework or N1/N2 input
adapter. Keep the existing two-distinct-peer API and its one-query contract.
The additive checked surface is `SeriesConfig::new(peer, request_timeout,
series_timeout)`, optional already-checked experimental profile attachment,
`prepare_series(&SeriesConfig)` and consuming
`execute_series(PreparedSeries, &ControlSchedule)`. Config/prepared/report fields
remain private, without raw/default/deserialization/mutation bypasses. A sole
fallible constructor invokes adopted Garde rules for new raw configuration and
token candidates. Source/exchange identity is created by actual preparation and
execution, never accepted as matching caller labels.

**Preparation and bounds.** Accept the existing loopback/nonzero-port/address
scope rules. Both durations are positive, at most the existing laboratory
`MAX_TIMEOUT` (30s), and request timeout cannot exceed series timeout. Preparation
reads one24-byte entropy buffer for three distinct nonzero eight-byte tokens,
rejecting zero/duplicate tokens without entropy retry or send. It creates no
socket queue. Execution uses one private current-thread runtime and at most one
active socket/request, one49-byte receive buffer and three owned terminal records.
Bind/register a fresh request socket sequentially; record setup failure as a
terminal series cause rather than silently replacing/retrying it. All remaining
planned attempts then remain explicitly not started. Each request allows at most
the existing64 readiness-driven send attempts and64 selected native datagrams,
with one confirmed query send and no retransmission. Whole-series limits are
192 such send attempts,192 selected datagrams and three confirmed sends.

**Time and identity.** Capture one actual `Instant` origin before runtime/native
setup. Total deadline is checked `origin + series_timeout`; each actual request
has checked `min(request_start + request_timeout, total_deadline)`. Later setup,
waiting or reevaluation never resets the total deadline. Each request has a fresh
actual request/socket owner, its prepared unique correlation token and the same
immutable configured-source/series context. Reports retain that shared context
and the actual `TransportFacts`, without native handles. New series have distinct
contexts even at the same address. Do not rearm a `Done` slot or call the old
two-peer `execute` repeatedly and relabel its distinct origins as one series.
This limited source/configuration identity establishes neither authenticated
server identity nor independent votes, production clock/mapping generations or
calibrated UTC accuracy. Existing aggregate control epoch remains laboratory
control, not a substitute for those unavailable production suppliers.

**State and completion.** The exclusive owner checks latest stop/invalidation,
deadline and request state before send, after selected I/O, and again immediately
before reply commit. Completion transfer is a private move from the actual
terminal request into the matching series/request position, checked against
actual owner identity and current control epoch. Duplicate/cross-series/stale
transfer cannot overwrite prior records or revive a request. Correlated
ordinary-header completion consumes its request even when nominal measurement
is refused. Preserve that irreversible retirement independently of downstream
numerical acceptance: rejection preserves earlier observation/history payloads
but never restores `Pending`. Malformed or wrong-origin datagrams consume the
bounded receive budget and may precede a valid neighboring reply; they do not
become completed measurements. Stop, invalidation or total deadline retires the
active request and marks later attempts not started, without extra sends.

**Historical meaning and cleanup.** Preserve real capture brackets, acquisition
and processing times, endpoints, raw signed RTT, corrected rational phase and
the exact half-nanosecond midpoint representation. Retain original acquisition
age; comparing views or choosing another anchor never makes an old result fresh.
Keep original date/model declarations and typed measurement refusal. No raw
clock reading, LI=0, host date or consistency tolerance supplies true-date/scale,
calendar, capture/mapping bound, quantum, prior frequency interval, quality
distance or unit weight. Those suppliers, NTS, statistical uncertainty/expiry/
readiness and production history remain held. Do not construct N1 rows or a
production `ValidatedSample`. Numerical report ownership remains the later
private transaction problem identified above, not copied N2 scalar certificates.
Retire/drop the current socket before preparing the next; reap any owned stop/
invalidation timers and drop the runtime before return, preserving cleanup errors
alongside a primary failure. Rust drop/host completion is not native Windows
handle/AFD release qualification.

**Independent acceptance and finite execution.** Through the real loopback entry,
observe three requests to the same destination, pairwise distinct actual exchange
identities/tokens, one common-origin reconstruction and no fourth send. Challenge
an old reply replayed at the next socket, malformed-then-valid neighboring replies,
nominal refusal consuming exactly once, cross-series/duplicate private completion
transfer, control/deadline between staging and commit, partial series failures,
receive/send caps, retained earlier records and honest age. Retain independent
half-tick and midpoint oscillator facts (-1/1024 versus receipt-labelled -5/6144)
where actual captures support them; exact scalar fixtures supplement rather than
replace actual producer observations. Add legal-use and external compile-fail
construction/mutation/consumption checks. Run focused development controls first,
then existing configured ordinary/two-target checks with six build jobs. Register
new critical owners and required test identities before freezing qualification;
reuse current admitted analysis budgets and per-file90% line/95% viable-mutation
gates, full inventories and alternative controls for ungenerated behavior. No
solver, new runner framework, broad source survey or optional tool work is needed.
The exit qualifies this controlled acquisition/receiver slice only; full Core
composition, native/NTS/service/clock/package/release gates and all holds remain.

### Final series-summary checkpoint repair — 8 November 2026

David requested removing the reporting ambiguity in `run_series` after analysis
of `89f035e`. This bounded N3 continuation belongs to the existing state,
publication and lifecycle contracts under CHECK-15/23/25/26; it changes no
production admission, native Windows permission, source identity or clock effect.
The unrelated `scripts/cross-link-service-fixture.py` edit remains outside scope.

Selected contract: historical completion transfer uses the request's actual
terminal snapshot. After transfer, the owner samples current control and time
under one short watch read guard and selects the summary before releasing that
guard. Stop precedes invalidation, which precedes the original total deadline.
When all three positions have transferred, an active checkpoint selects
`Completed`. Control updates serialized after this checkpoint do not revise the
selected summary or historical records. Cleanup failures remain separately
observable and may promote the result to `Failure`.

Plan and detecting checks:

1. Add a private current-summary helper and notification-only Before/After
   checkpoints around actual post-transfer classification. Production passes an
   empty observer; callbacks run outside the watch guard and cannot supply the
   classification or a completion. Preserve the old post-transfer decision long
   enough to obtain a behavioral RED with the new deterministic test.
2. Use actual three-request `run_series` fixtures and bounded external control
   acknowledgements: stop/invalidation before the final checkpoint select
   `Stopped`/`Stale`; either after it preserves `Completed`. Check all three
   retained reports and original facts/age, exactly three queries and no fourth.
   Add reached-deadline controls for current stop/invalidation precedence without
   timing sleeps; retain existing between-commit-and-transfer coverage.
3. Wire the fresh post-transfer helper, establish GREEN, then format and freeze
   the final candidate. Run configured ordinary compiler/Clippy/tests/docs and
   Windows-target checks, plus affected critical-file qualification. Historical
   N3 receipts retain their exact source and do not qualify this changed owner.
4. Independently review the final implementation and source-bound observations;
   record actual results, any incomplete gates and the next action here.
